Abstract: One-Time Passwords (OTPs) have become a common option for multi-factor authentication in several applications. For instance, during website login processes, OTPs are often used in conjunction with traditional text-based usernames and passwords to verify whether the access request originates from a legitimate human user rather than an automated agent. However, in scenarios involving automated connections and system-to-system interoperability, Time-Based One-Time Passwords (TOTPs) may be required to establish secure connections and access Web Services (WSs). Therefore, this study focuses on exploring the development of a quantum-safe web service architecture. The proposed approach achieves transmission security management by implementing Transport Layer Security (TLS) and HyperText Transfer Protocol Secure (HTTPS) based on Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC). Furthermore, web service security management is realized through the construction of keyed-Hash Message Authentication Code (HMAC)-driven TOTPs. Within the experimental environment, this study evaluates and compares the computational performance of the Secure Hash Algorithm-2 (SHA-2), SHA-3, Ascon-Hash256, and SM3. The required computation time under different hardware resource conditions is analyzed for future web service deployment.

</details>


### [230] [Probing the Prefill: Detecting Code Vulnerabilities via Latent Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16970)
*Alizishaan Khatri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在处理C/C++代码时，其隐藏激活是否已经蕴含代码漏洞状态的信息。作者从三个模型家族的四个LLM中提取最后一层prefill token激活，训练MLP探针进行分类。探针参数量仅为基础模型的0.2%以下，在Devign基准上达到68.8% F1，与微调分类器SOTA（67.9%）持平，但在其他更难、更不平衡的基准上表现落后。这初步证明编码LLM自身的代码表示对漏洞状态具有判别性信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代码生成漏洞防御都是事后的（post-hoc）：静态分析器、微调分类器或LLM裁判在生成的代码完成后才进行筛查，忽略了生成模型自身的内部状态。作者希望探究一个更直接可测量的问题——LLM读取代码时，其隐藏激活是否已经携带了代码漏洞状态的信息信号，从而为轻量级、模型原生（model-native）的漏洞筛查提供可能。

Method: 1) 从四个LLM（Granite-4.1-8B, Qwen3.5-9B, Qwen3.6-27B, Gemma-4-12B）中提取prefill阶段最后一层的token激活；2) 在这些激活上训练MLP探针（probe）；3) 在四个函数级C/C++基准（Devign, Big-Vul, Draper VDISC, PrimeVul）上评估探针性能。探针参数量为13.4-16.0M，不到基础模型规模的0.2%。

Result: 探针平均F1为41.7%。在Devign上，最佳探针（Qwen3.5-9B，68.8% F1）匹配甚至略超已发布的微调分类器SOTA（67.9% F1），且仅需读取冻结通用LLM的激活。但在更难、更不平衡的基准（Big-Vul, Draper VDISC, PrimeVul）上，探针与SOTA差距显著。

Conclusion: 这是初步证据表明编码LLM对任意代码的内部表示包含关于该代码漏洞状态的有用信息。尽管在困难基准上仍有差距，但该结果启发未来对轻量级、模型原生的漏洞筛查方向的进一步研究。

Abstract: LLM-based code generation is now embedded in mission-critical pipelines, but defenses against vulnerable output remain post-hoc -- static analyzers, fine-tuned classifiers, or an LLM judge that screen completed code, ignoring the generating model's own internal state. We test a narrower, directly measurable question: when an LLM reads a piece of C/C++ code as context, do its hidden activations already carry a signal about that code's vulnerability status? We extract last prefill token activations from four LLMs (Granite-4.1-8B, Qwen3.5-9B, Qwen3.6-27B, Gemma-4-12B) across three model families and train MLP probes on these activations. We evaluate them on four function-level C/C++ benchmarks (Devign, Big-Vul, Draper VDISC, PrimeVul). Our probes achieve 41.7\% average F1 using 13.4--16.0M-parameter probes -- under 0.2\% of base-model size. On Devign, the best probe (Qwen3.5-9B, 68.8\% F1) matches the published fine-tuned-classifier SOTA (67.9\%) despite reading only a frozen, general-purpose LLM's activations; on the harder, more imbalanced benchmarks (Big-Vul, Draper VDISC, PrimeVul) probes trail SOTA substantially. This is early evidence that a coding LLM's own representation of arbitrary code is informative about that code's vulnerability status, motivating further work toward lightweight, model-native vulnerability screening.

</details>


### [231] [Remote-Timer-as-a-Service: Efficient Microarchitectural Leakage in the Cloud with Remote Timers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17043)
*Martin Schwarzl,Haocheng Xiao,Albert Pedersen,Sam Ainsworth,Nigel Topham*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示Cloudflare Workers生产环境中DyPrIs缓解机制存在缺陷，通过微架构放大技术成功实现了远程Spectre攻击，以99.16%的准确率、每秒12比特的速度窃取共置worker的JWT令牌，最终促使Cloudflare采用V8沙箱、增强DyPrIs检测和MPK进程内隔离进行修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cloudflare Workers为降低启动延迟移除进程隔离边界，采用语言级隔离架构，这带来了Spectre攻击风险。虽然此前已部署DyPrIs等缓解措施，但研究者怀疑其生产实现存在不足，需要验证并揭示安全漏洞。

Method: 采用微架构放大技术发现生产环境中多种计时可能性，利用计时放大和远程定时器组合，在Cloudflare Workers生产环境中实施远程Spectre攻击，从共置的受害者worker中窃取JWT令牌，并以端到端攻击验证DyPrIs缓解机制的缺陷。

Result: 攻击成功以99.16%的准确率和最高12 bit/s的速度窃取JWT令牌，相比现有攻击（2 bit/min）性能提升数个数量级，对客户数据构成即时风险。

Conclusion: DyPrIs的生产实现不足以阻止Spectre攻击，冻结和粗化定时器无法有效防御。Cloudflare在协调披露后采用V8沙箱限制瞬态访问为64位指针、改进DyPrIs检测能力，并部署基于MPK的硬件辅助进程内隔离来缓解攻击。

Abstract: Edge computing solutions have become a crucial part of the industry, delivering fast, flexible and scalable applications close to the end users, with typical use cases including dynamic content creation, image resizing and chatbots. Cloudflare Workers is one such framework, which handles millions of HTTP requests per second worldwide. To reduce start-up latency, Cloudflare Workers removes process-isolation boundaries between multiple tenants and leverages language-level isolation. This architecture poses the risk of Spectre attacks. To mitigate these, Cloudflare Workers previously introduced several countermeasures such as restricted timer measurements, no shared memory, no multithreading and Dynamic Process Isolation (DyPrIs), detecting potential attacks and process-isolating potentially malicious scripts.
  We demonstrate that the production implementation of DyPrIs was insufficient. We adopt microarchitectural amplification techniques and discover various possibilities to measure time in the production environment of Cloudflare Workers. Given these techniques, we show that freezing and coarsening timers in the Cloudflare Workers security model is insufficient. Leveraging both timing amplification and remote timers, we demonstrate a remote Spectre attack that leaks a JWT token from a co-located victim worker in the Cloudflare Workers production environment. We outperform the existing attack by orders of magnitude, going from 2 bit/min to up to 12 bit/s at an accuracy of 99.16%, posing an immediate risk to customer data. Following our end-to-end attack, Cloudflare Workers mitigated it in a coordinated effort by integrating the V8 Sandbox limiting transient access to 64-bit pointers, improving the detection capabilities of DyPrIs, and deploying hardware-assisted MPK-based in-process isolation to confine each tenant heap under a dedicated memory-protection key.

</details>


### [232] [CUSTOS: Toward Forensic-Ready Zero Trust at the Capture-Containment Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17068)
*Avinash Srinivasan,John Paramadilok*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出CUSTOS，一个面向取证就绪的零信任参考架构，以取证管理点（FMP）为核心，协调分层捕获、身份与策略关联的重建、遥测编排及受控调查访问。实验表明始终在线的决策记录（哈希链）以1.9-3.0%的吞吐量代价保留决策来源，但反应式捕获在对抗自我销毁场景下失效，身份导向遥测显著优于网络导向遥测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零信任架构通过持续验证取代隐式信任，但相互TLS、临时工作负载、身份中心控制和自动化修复导致负载可见性降低、基于IP的归因减弱、获取易失性证据的时间窗口缩小，从而使取证能力受损。

Method: 提出CUSTOS参考架构，核心是取证管理点（FMP），实现分层捕获、身份与策略关联重建、遥测编排和零信任控制的调查访问。原型采用实时强制网关加独立运行时与编排器实验，包括网关哈希链决策记录、反应式检查点、容器捕获与SIGKILL排序测试，以及多个公开基准数据集上的遥测模式对比。

Result: 始终在线决策记录在进程内策略引擎上仅产生1.9-3.0%吞吐量开销；反应式检查点约65ms，但落后于非排序直接SIGKILL（约9ms）、内核强制和对抗性自我销毁，产生取证碎片化效应；在真实容器中，并发捕获与SIGKILL恢复秘密的成功率为0/1000，而将SIGKILL排在FMP屏障之后成功率为1000/1000；身份导向遥测填充决策记录模式的64-75%，远高于网络导向的18-30%。

Conclusion: 取证就绪的零信任需要同时具备始终在线的证据基线和有界的反应式捕获，同时应明确识别易失性证据仍无法恢复的场景，为未来取证架构设计提供指导。

Abstract: Zero Trust (ZT) replaces implicit trust with continuous verification, but mutual TLS, ephemeral workloads, identity-centric control, and automated remediation reduce payload visibility, weaken IP-based attribution, and shrink the window for acquiring volatile evidence. We propose CUSTOS, a forensic-ready ZT reference architecture centered on a Forensic Management Point (FMP) that coordinates tiered capture, identity- and policy-linked reconstruction, telemetry orchestration, and ZT-controlled investigative access. We evaluate a composed, component-level prototype using a live enforcement gateway plus separate runtime and orchestrator experiments. An always-on decision record is captured and hash-chained on the gateway at a 1.9-3.0\% throughput cost on in-process policy engines, preserving decision provenance outside the monitored workload under stated trust assumptions. Reactive checkpointing (about 65 ms) precedes seconds-scale defender-routed eviction but loses to unsequenced direct SIGKILL (about 9 ms), in-kernel enforcement, and adversarial self-destruction, producing the forensic shredder effect. On a real container, concurrent capture and SIGKILL recovered the planted secret in 0/1000 trials; sequencing SIGKILL behind the FMP barrier recovered it in 1000/1000. The primary integrated single-node Kubernetes race checkpoints an FMP-controlled process; container-memory capture is evaluated separately and was unavailable in the managed-Kubernetes configuration. Across five public benchmark datasets and a synthetic schema reference, identity-oriented telemetry populates 64-75\% of the decision-record schema against 18-30\% for network-oriented, while rate limiting bounds the full-memory admission ceiling. These results show that forensic-ready ZT requires both an always-on evidentiary floor and bounded reactive capture, while identifying where volatile evidence remains unrecoverable.

</details>


### [233] [SentryBus: A Multi-Vantage Observability Model and Validated Instrument for I2C Sensor-Interface Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17082)
*Sandesh More,Elton Batista,Karla Daley,Sneha Sudhakaran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出SentryBus系统，通过在I2C传感器总线上被动监测主机侧总线，利用事务时序、读写序列、传输长度、地址行为、寄存器/FIFO状态及原始数据转换等特征，检测传感器接口被篡改（如内联插接器、并行控制器、传感器替换或主机被攻陷）的可观测证据。实验表明内联插接器被限制在采集服务时间的0.842%，6304秒内无时钟回归，但数据转换特征不具有跨会话迁移性，且攻击试验尚未完成，未声称检出率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有传感器驱动系统（医疗IoT、无人机、网络物理系统）默认测量值到达嵌入式处理器后即被信任，但数字接口上的攻击者可提供看似合理却被固件接受并上报的虚假遥测数据，缺乏对该攻击途径的系统性检测方案。

Method: 提出SentryBus宿主侧被动监视器，对I2C传感器总线的事务时序、读写序列、传输长度、地址行为、寄存器/FIFO状态访问和原始数据转换进行建模；将对手建模为内联插接器、并行控制器、传感器替换或主机被攻陷四类；搭建双端测试台，宿主侧总线供检测器使用，其他视角作为真值。

Result: 三项实测结果：内联插接器在保持采集调度和负载内容时被限制在采集服务时间的0.842%；清洁采集在遥测视角持续6304秒无时钟回归；数据转换特征编码了会话特定信号统计量，不跨会话迁移。仪器表征显示低成本分析器可在内核无可见错误的情况下截断采集。

Conclusion: 传感器接口操纵会在采集路径上留下可观测证据，且证据随攻击者位置在不同视角呈现差异；被动宿主侧监视存在可测量的操纵不可区分边界，但受攻击性试验尚未完成，检出率有待后续验证。

Abstract: Sensor-driven systems in medical Internet of Things devices, drones, and cyber-physical systems commonly trust a measurement once it reaches the embedded processor. An adversary on the digital interface between sensor and processor can supply a plausible value that correct firmware accepts and reports as ordinary telemetry. The hypothesis is that sensor interface manipulation leaves observable evidence on the acquisition path, that the evidence appears at different vantages depending on attacker position, and that a passive host-side monitor therefore has a measurable boundary beyond which manipulation becomes indistinguishable from legitimate acquisition. SentryBus models acquisition behavior on the I2C sensor bus using transaction timing, read and write sequences, transfer lengths, address behavior, register and FIFO state access, and raw data transitions. The adversary is modeled as an inline interposer, parallel controller, sensor replacement, or compromised host, because a commodity target-only sensor cannot initiate transfers or stretch, reorder, or delay bus transactions. A dual sided testbed captures both busses, host memory, and telemetry, and the detector consumes the host facing bus alone while the remaining vantages serve as ground truth. A physiological instantiation reports three measured results: an inline interposer bounded at 0.842 percent of acquisition service time while preserving acquisition schedule and payload content, clean acquisition stability sustained over 6304 seconds at the telemetry vantage with no clock regression, and a negative result establishing that data-transition features encode session specific signal statistics and do not transfer across capture sessions. Instrument characterization shows that a low-cost analyzer can truncate captures without kernel visible error. Controlled attack trials are still outstanding, so no detection rate is claimed.

</details>


### [234] [Structured Driving-State Narratives for Small Language Model-Based GNSS Spoofing Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17092)
*Abyad Enan,Sagar Dasgupta,Mizanur Rahman,Mashrur Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于小型语言模型（SLM）的框架，用于检测和分类自动驾驶车辆中的GNSS欺骗攻击。该框架将来自GNSS和其他传感源的独立驾驶状态转换为结构化语义叙述，输入SLM进行判断，在精度上与大型语言模型（LLM）相当（平均准确率96.99%），同时大幅降低了推理延迟和GPU内存占用，适用于资源受限的车载计算平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆依赖GNSS定位，但欺骗信号可导致合理但错误的车辆状态。现有检测方法需要高效、实时且计算资源占用低的方案，以便在资源受限的车载平台上部署。

Method: 该框架将来自GNSS和其他传感源的独立驾驶状态转化为结构化语义叙述，提供给SLM进行欺骗检测与攻击分类。评估包含五类场景（无攻击、过冲攻击、停车攻击、逐步转向攻击、错误转弯攻击），并与在相同训练数据上微调的LLM进行对比，同时使用地理上未见过的实地数据（南卡罗来纳州克莱姆森采集）进行验证。

Result: SLM与LLM性能相当，平均准确率96.99%、精确率99.05%、召回率95.59%、F1分数97.18%。SLM在推理延迟和GPU内存（微调及推理阶段）方面均优于LLM，且地理迁移的实地数据验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够以较低的计算和内存资源实现实时GNSS欺骗攻击检测与分类，适合部署在资源受限的车载计算平台上。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) depend on reliable Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) positioning. However, spoofed GNSS signals can induce plausible but incorrect vehicle states. This study develops a small language model (SLM)-based framework for detecting and classifying GNSS spoofing attacks by comparing vehicle behaviors independently derived from GNSS and other sensing sources. The framework converts independent driving states from GNSS and other sensing sources into structured semantic narratives that are provided to an SLM for spoofing detection and attack classification. The performance of the SLM-based framework is compared with large language models (LLMs) fine-tuned on identical training data and evaluated on the same test set. The evaluation considers five classes: no attack, overshoot attack, stopped attack, turn-by-turn attack, and wrong-turn attack. The framework is also evaluated with geographically unseen field data collected in Clemson, South Carolina, United States. Experimental results indicate that the evaluated SLMs achieve performance similar to the LLMs, achieving an average accuracy of 96.99%, precision of 99.05%, recall of 95.59%, and F1-score of 97.18%. In terms of computational efficiency and resource utilization, the SLMs demonstrate advantages over the LLMs by requiring lower inference latency and less GPU memory during both fine-tuning and inference. Evaluation using field data collected in a geographically distinct location further demonstrated its efficacy. The presented framework can detect and classify GNSS spoofing attacks in real-time while requiring relatively low computational and memory resources, and is therefore suitable for deployment on resource-constrained vehicular computing platforms.

</details>


### [235] [Digital Twin-Based Intrusion Detection for Vehicle Powertrain CAN Bus Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17093)
*Araf Rahman,M Sabbir Salek,Mashrur Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于数字孪生（DT）的汽车CAN总线入侵检测系统，利用共享编码器LSTM对17个解码后的动力总成信号进行联合建模，通过预测与观测行为的残差来识别攻击。实验表明该方法在持续漂移和伪装攻击上分别达到94.6%和89.2%的检测率，优于传统基线方法，但存在39.6%的误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有汽车CAN入侵检测系统主要关注消息时序、频率或序列的异常，无法检测在保持这些通信属性不变的情况下篡改有效载荷的攻击。数字孪生此前仅用于CAN流量仿真和攻击场景生成，尚未被用于入侵检测，本文填补了这一空白。

Method: 开发了一个基于数字孪生的IDS，采用共享编码器LSTM对17个解码后的动力总成信号进行联合预测（7个数值信号和2个类别档位信号），预测窗口为24步。通过预测与观测行为之间的残差是否超过校准阈值来标记异常时间步；同时采用自适应滚动机制保护孪生模型输入历史不被持续污染。评估了四种攻击类型（平台攻击、连续漂移、伪装攻击和档位伪装攻击）。

Result: DT模型在所有攻击类型上均优于范围和合理性基线方法：连续漂移攻击检测率94.6%，伪装攻击检测率89.2%，而基线对大多数伪造载荷攻击几乎无法检测。但误报率高达39.6%，表明在持续攻击下鲁棒性仍需改进。

Conclusion: 学习耦合的车辆动力学关系能够有效检测保持正常CAN通信模式的隐蔽载荷操纵攻击，基于数字孪生的IDS为联网及自动驾驶汽车的基于行为的网络安全提供了有前景的方向，但需进一步降低误报率以提升实际部署的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Existing automotive intrusion detection systems (IDSs) for the Controller Area Network (CAN) largely target discrepancies in message timing, frequency, or sequencing and cannot detect attacks that preserve these properties while manipulating the payload. Digital twins (DTs) have been used to emulate CAN traffic and generate attack scenarios for IDS evaluation, but their use for intrusion detection remains unexplored. This study develops a DT-based IDS that jointly models physical relationships among decoded powertrain signals and identifies attacks through residuals between predicted and observed behavior. A shared-encoder LSTM DT was trained on 17 decoded signals from a real Hyundai/Kia CAN log to jointly predict seven numeric and two categorical gear signals over a 24-step window. A timestep is flagged when a residual exceeds a calibrated threshold, while adaptive rollout protects the twin's input history from sustained contamination. Four attacks (plateau, continuous drift, masquerade, and gear masquerade) were evaluated against the twin and a range-and-plausibility baseline. The DT outperformed the baseline across all attacks, achieving detection rates of 94.6% for continuous drift and 89.2% for masquerade, while the baseline detected almost none of the fabricated payload attacks. These results demonstrate that learning coupled vehicle dynamics enables detection of stealthy payload manipulations that preserve normal CAN communication patterns. False positive rates reached 39.6%, highlighting the need for improved robustness under sustained attacks. The DT-based IDS shows promise for detecting stealthy payload-level CAN attacks that preserve normal communication patterns, supporting behavior-based cybersecurity for connected and automated vehicles.

</details>


### [236] [Protocol-Embedded Compliance for Privacy-Preserving, Non-Custodial Digital Payments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17145)
*Santiago De Simone,Geoffrey Goodell,Georgios Samakovitis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套非托管、类现金的数字资产参考架构与核心协议规则，将合规执行嵌入协议层而非依赖机构托管或基于身份账户控制，在保护用户隐私的同时实现强审计性，并论证了其可与现有支付系统对接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为支付基础设施必须采用托管型、账户制模型才能保障交易完整性、审计性和合规性（尤其是反洗钱要求）。本文旨在挑战这一假设，论证非托管、类现金的数字资产同样能够体现这些能力，且可能更安全。

Method: 基于Goodell等人提出的不可伪造、有状态、遗忘型（USO）资产模型，设计了一个数字价值交换系统的参考架构和核心协议规则。将监管合规数据以加密签名证明的形式嵌入资产状态，由独立实体签发；交易仅在满足合规谓词并在资产状态中纳入相应签名后才有效，从而在协议层面实现合规执行。

Result: 研究表明，通过将合规数据和签名证明嵌入资产状态，可以在不依赖机构托管或身份账户控制的情况下实现协议级合规执行，同时保持用户隐私和强审计性。该模型能够与现有支付系统有效对接，支持非托管合规验证交易与传统金融基础设施的整合。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：非托管的合规验证数字交易模型不仅可行，而且可以与遗留金融基础设施成功接口，为受监管的数字货币交换提供了一条不依赖机构托管的替代路径。

Abstract: Received wisdom on payments infrastructure strongly supports the custodial, account-based model as a necessity for transaction integrity, auditability and verification; the set of fundamental primitives for regulated digital money exchange, the argument goes, necessitates designated identifiable entities that store and process credentials, perform KYC, and ultimately act as the 'single version of the truth' for compliance remediation and, most important, AML. In this paper, we propose this is not the case, by arguing that non-custodial, cash-like digital assets can embody such capabilities, in an arguably more secure manner.
  To that end, we present a reference architecture and core protocol rules for digital-value-exchange systems that preserve meaningful user privacy while enabling strong auditability. The protocol defines the conditions under which digital asset creation, transfer, and redemption are valid. The architecture specifies the allocation of actors, roles and components through which these rules operate, enabling independent verification of transaction compliance with applicable norms. Building upon the Unforgeable, Stateful, Oblivious (USO) asset model of Goodell et al., regulatory compliance data are embedded directly into the asset state as cryptographically signed attestations issued by independent entities. A transfer is valid only upon satisfaction of applicable compliance predicates and inclusion of the resulting signature within the asset state. Compliance enforcement is thus performed at the protocol level rather than through institutional custody or identity-based account control. We conclude that our proposed model can successfully interface with existing payment systems, making it possible to integrate non-custodial, compliance-verified transactions with legacy financial infrastructure.

</details>


### [237] [Picture the Epsilon: Pursuing Identity-Level Privacy Guarantees for Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17147)
*Arman Zareian Jahromi,Vishnu Bondalakunta,Mohammad Akbar Bin Shah,Naimul Haque,Shuangqing Wei,George T. Amariucai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对四种面向黑盒人脸生成器的身份级差分隐私审计方法进行了比较研究，在FaceFusion和InstantID等系统上的实验表明，这些方法在高度可区分性场景下均能揭示显著的个体可辨别性，但由于假设和有限样本处理方式不同，得到的epsilon估计差异显著，无法进行可靠排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸生成器的视觉不相似性常被误认为隐私保护的证据，但缺乏系统性的身份级差分隐私审计框架来验证其正式隐私保证。如何将嵌入空间观测转化为epsilon估计存在多种不同路径，需要对这些审计方法进行比较研究。

Method: 论文提出了四种审计方法：(1)基于高斯机制和个体敏感度的方法(GaussMech)；(2)基于逐维核密度对数比并通过基本组合聚合的方法(KDE-LR)；(3)基于最大均值差异经全变差距离推导纯DP epsilon下界的解析方法(MMD-TV)；(4)基于交叉验证分类器ROC的假设检验方法(ROC-HT)。每种方法都明确了其假设、超参数依赖和有限样本限制。

Result: 在FaceFusion和InstantID上使用多种身份编码器和参考数据集进行实验，四种审计方法均一致揭示了显著的个体可辨别性，但报告的epsilon估计值差异明显，反映了各自不同的假设和有限样本处理方法。在高度可区分性场景下，实验不支持对这四种方法进行可靠排序。

Conclusion: 四种审计方法的相对权衡应在部分隐私机制上评估，这是下一步自然的研究方向。该框架将审计方法置于统一身份级审计设置中，阐明了其假设和有限样本处理如何影响差分隐私估计结果。

Abstract: Image-to-image face generators are widely used, and visual dissimilarity between their outputs and source images is sometimes treated as evidence of privacy. Auditing whether these systems satisfy formal identity-level (epsilon, delta)-differential privacy requires choosing among several distinct routes for converting embedding-space observations into estimates or bounds on the differential privacy parameter epsilon. We present a comparative study of four such audits applicable to pre-trained, black-box face generators: a Gaussian-mechanism reading of per-identity sensitivity (GaussMech); a per-dimension kernel-density log-ratio aggregated by basic composition (KDE-LR); an analytical population-level lower bound on pure-DP epsilon derived from the maximum mean discrepancy via the total variation distance (MMD-TV); and a hypothesis-testing evaluation of a cross-validated classifier's out-of-fold ROC (ROC-HT). For each method we make explicit its assumptions, hyperparameter dependence, finite-sample limitations, and the regime in which its epsilon estimate is informative. Applied to FaceFusion and InstantID across multiple identity encoders and reference datasets, the audits consistently reveal substantial identity distinguishability while reporting markedly different epsilon estimates that reflect each method's distinct assumptions and finite-sample treatment. In this high-distinguishability regime, the experiments do not support a reliable ranking of the four methods. Their relative trade-offs should be evaluated on partially private mechanisms, which we identify as the natural next study. The resulting framework places these audits in a shared identity-level audit setting and clarifies how their assumptions and finite-sample treatments shape the resulting differential privacy estimates.

</details>


### [238] [Authorization Before Context: A Model-Neutral Audience Boundary Against Cross-Audience Memory Leakage in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17148)
*Sibo Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对个人语言智能体在"记忆→上下文"转换环节存在的隐私攻击面（跨受众窥探、渠道歧义、记忆投毒等），提出了"先授权、后组上下文"的边界规则：基于反单调的受众成员判定，在将记忆项放入提示词之前执行排除性检查，确保任何被禁止的事实都不会进入最终上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人语言智能体从某一受众处习得事实后，可能在另一受众的提示词构造中复用它。这一"记忆到上下文"的步骤构成了攻击面：渠道歧义、跨受众偷窥以及记忆投毒都可能使系统组装出对当前观看者未经授权的上下文内容，需要引入机制层面的防御而非依赖模型行为。

Method: 提出"授权先于上下文"原则：在记忆到上下文转换处应用单一的反单调受众成员规则。每条记忆携带其记录时的受众集合；当前观看者集合从渠道元数据读取，歧义时回退为公开；仅当所有当前观看者均属于该记忆的受众时才允许该记忆进入上下文。该规则通过排除机制而非模型约束来保证更窄受众的信息不会泄露给更广受众，且投毒记忆无法自我扩大受众范围。

Result: 该边界是对精确组装上下文的一种模型无关不变量：在调用模型之前，被禁止的事实必须不存在于上下文中。在合成的情境完整性（Contextual-Integrity）测试集上，所提出的边界未使任何被禁止事实进入其组装的上下文，而无范围的基线按构造包含了此类事实；进一步审计确认所有读取路径均失败关闭。

Conclusion: 初步合成证据表明该授权边界在跨渠道回忆保障与信息隔离之间取得了平衡，但证据仍属初步且基于合成数据，后续需要在更真实场景下验证。

Abstract: A personal language agent learns a fact from one audience and may later place it in the prompt it assembles for another. This memory-to-context step is an attack surface: ambiguous or inconsistent channels, cross-audience prying, and poisoned memory can each cause the system to assemble context containing a fact relevant to the query yet unauthorized for the current viewers. We introduce authorization before context: a single, anti-monotone audience-membership rule applied at the memory-to-context transition. Each item carries the audience present when it was recorded; the current viewer set is read from channel metadata and falls back to public when ambiguous; and the item is admitted only when every current viewer already belonged to its audience. We prove that this rule gives every participant cross-channel recall while ensuring, by exclusion rather than by model behavior, that nothing recorded for a narrower audience reaches a broader one and that poisoned memory cannot widen its own audience. The boundary is a model-neutral invariant on the exact assembled context: a forbidden fact must be absent before the model is called. On a synthetic Contextual-Integrity suite, no forbidden fact entered the context our boundary assembled, whereas unscoped baselines included such facts by construction; we further audit that every read path fails closed. The evidence is preliminary and synthetic.

</details>


### [239] [Beyond the Hype: Evaluating LLM Integration and Practical Limitations in Security Operation Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17154)
*Elnaz Rabieinejad,Ali Dehghantanha,Fattane Zarrinkalam,Sarina Dastgerdy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对20名SOC从业者的半结构化访谈，揭示了LLM在安全运营中心中的实际使用现状：从业者认可其在低风险、可快速验证任务上的效率提升，但对其在高风险安全决策中的可靠性、推理透明度和验证成本持谨慎态度。作者基于访谈证据提出了LLM集成就绪度的成熟度评估框架，并规划了以可审计性和透明解释机制为核心的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被越来越多地引入安全运营中心（SOC）用于文本密集型分析工作（如告警上下文化、事件摘要、调查文书起草），但从业者普遍担忧幻觉问题（看似合理但错误的输出）、推理不透明以及验证成本。现有研究缺乏对一线SOC实践者的系统化定性研究，本文旨在填补这一空白，深入理解LLM在真实安全工作流中的实际价值与风险。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈的定性研究方法，访谈对象为20名SOC从业者，涵盖一线分析师、SOC管理者和工具开发者。通过主题分析提炼从业者对LLM使用体验、信任程度、验证实践及安全担忧的核心发现，并在此基础上构建成熟度评估框架。

Result: 研究发现：1）从业者认可LLM在低风险、可快速验证任务（如日志摘要、初步调查线索起草）中带来感知上的时间节省；2）从业者普遍将LLM输出视为初步草稿或建议，而非决策级别的结论；3）LLM输出的不可靠性和推理不透明导致其在高风险安全决策中信任度有限；4）当前依赖临时性的验证规范与持续人工监督，而非标准化的缓解流程。

Conclusion: 作者提出了评估SOC对LLM集成就绪度的成熟度评估框架，并强调未来研究应聚焦于可审计性与透明的解释机制，以促进LLM在安全运营工作流中的安全采纳。研究指出了从非标准化、临时验证向系统化保障机制过渡的必要性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being explored within Security Operation Centers (SOCs) to support text-heavy analytical work such as alert contextualization, incident summarization, and drafting investigative artifacts. Despite this interest, practitioners describe critical operational concerns, most notably hallucinations (plausible but incorrect outputs), opaque reasoning, and the verification effort required to safely use model-generated content in security workflows. In this paper, we present findings from semi-structured interviews with 20 SOC practitioners spanning frontline analysts, SOC managers, and tool developers. Participants report perceived time savings for low-stakes tasks that are quickly verifiable (e.g., summarizing logs or drafting initial investigative leads), but they consistently frame LLM outputs as preliminary drafts and suggestions rather than decision-grade conclusions. Participants also describe limited trust in LLMs for high-stakes security decisions due to unreliable outputs and unclear model reasoning, and they report relying primarily on ad-hoc verification norms and continuous human oversight rather than standardized mitigation procedures. Based on these interview-grounded accounts, we introduce a maturity rubric to characterize readiness for LLM integration and outline a research agenda emphasizing auditability and transparent explanation mechanisms to support safer adoption in SOC workflows.

</details>


### [240] [The Acknowledgment Point Is the System: Durable Policy-Decision Receipts for AI Audit Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17176)
*Neeraj Kumar Singh Beshane*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文重构了RuntimeGuard-AI，一个AI审计记录系统，将每次确定性策略决策绑定到精确策略来源，在调用者选择的同步边界提交隐私最小化记录，并返回Ed25519签名的回执来明确durability是否完成。系统在M4 Pro上实现了缓冲签名证据27,193请求/秒、中位延迟141.9μs，而完全同步时降至约242请求/秒和16.0ms延迟。作者强调这是一个测量的durability-latency权衡而非"免费"的异步审计路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI审计记录只有在其持久性和信任边界明确时才是有用的。在持久写入前返回受保护的决策可以最小化延迟，但无法保证证据在立即崩溃后幸存。因此需要重建系统来明确这一约束，提供可验证的持久性保证。

Method: 系统将每次确定性策略决策绑定到精确策略来源，在调用者选择的同步边界提交隐私最小化的记录，并返回Ed25519签名的回执以说明边界是否完成。重启后引擎验证框架记录、清单、分片放置、序列连续性和重放身份。另有一条独立的认证路径将已提交记录分组为链式签名的Merkle纪元，审计员使用外部获得的密钥进行验证。

Result: 在Apple M4 Pro上，四工作线程、2,048字节提示的条件下，缓冲签名证据达到27,193请求/秒，中位延迟141.9微秒；每记录数据和完全同步将吞吐量降至约242请求/秒，中位延迟16.0毫秒；封印100,000条记录的签名纪元耗时97.0毫秒。

Conclusion: 研究结果明确了持久性与延迟之间存在的量化权衡，而非提供看似免费的异步审计路径。原型不证明模型执行、无法防止被攻陷的签名者分叉历史，也不确立法律合规性。

Abstract: An AI audit record is useful only if its durability and trust boundary are explicit. Returning a guarded decision before any durable write minimizes latency, but it cannot guarantee that evidence survives an immediate crash. We rebuild RuntimeGuard-AI around this constraint. The resulting research prototype binds each deterministic policy decision to the exact policy source, commits a privacy-minimizing record at a caller-selected synchronization boundary, and returns an Ed25519-signed receipt that states whether that boundary completed. After restart, the engine validates framed records, manifests, shard placement, sequence continuity, and replay identity. A separate attestation path groups committed records into chained, signed Merkle epochs that an auditor verifies with an externally obtained key. On an Apple M4 Pro at four worker threads and 2,048-byte prompts, buffered signed evidence reaches 27,193 requests/s with 141.9 microseconds median latency. Per-record data and full synchronization reduce throughput to approximately 242 requests/s and raise median latency to 16.0 ms. Sealing a 100,000-record signed epoch takes 97.0 ms. The result is a measured durability-latency trade-off, not a "free" asynchronous audit path. The prototype does not prove model execution, prevent a compromised signer from forking history, or establish legal conformity.

</details>


### [241] [PACE: Policy-Attested Contract Execution for Safe AI Agents in Decentralized Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17220)
*Rabimba Karanjai,Yang Lu,Richard Williamson,Hemanth Hm,Prakhar Mehrotra,Lei Xu,Weidong,Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了PACE（策略认证合约执行），一种在LLM代理与链上执行之间插入的事务级授权框架。PACE通过类型化事务意图、确定性策略验证器和签名策略决策记录（PDR），将批准的意图、策略和模拟报告加密绑定到精确的执行字节上，并带重放和过期保护。在确定性沙箱中，PACE实现了0.00%的不安全执行率和0.00%的良性任务误报率，与未受保护基线的0.80相比显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI代理正在成为DeFi操作的接口，但由于依赖LLM规划交易，它们继承了LLM对提示注入的脆弱性，且缺乏将验证者的批准绑定到最终链上提交的确切事务的机制。

Method: PACE引入类型化事务意图、确定性策略验证器和签名策略决策记录（PDR），通过Solidity智能账户在链上强制实施PDR签名。该框架在LLM代理和链上执行之间建立中间层，实现加密绑定、重放保护和过期保护。作者还提供了三个模型的实时LLM评估和主网分叉测试工具。

Result: 在2,800次试验（40个任务、4类攻击加良性场景、10个种子）中，PACE在确定性沙箱中实现0.00%的不安全执行率和0.00%的良性任务误报率，相比未受保护基线的0.80有显著改进。链上开销为29,826-31,822 gas。消融研究确定宽松策略设置（+57.5个百分点）和合约白名单（+12.5个百分点）是主要的安全组件。

Conclusion: 本文的声明限定在可复现基准内的逻辑级安全性，而非部署就绪的DeFi安全性。PACE为基于LLM的DeFi代理提供了事务级授权框架，在确定性基准中展现了优异的性能，但辅助研究（实时LLM评估和主网分叉测试）与确定性基准分离，不应作为替代。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents are emerging as interfaces for decentralized finance (DeFi) actions such as swaps, lending operations, and yield management. Because these agents rely on large language models (LLMs) to plan transactions, they inherit the LLM's susceptibility to prompt injection and lack of mechanisms to bind a verifier's approval to the exact transaction ultimately submitted on-chain. We present PACE (Policy-Attested Contract Execution), a transaction-level authorization framework that interposes between an LLM-based agent and on-chain execution. PACE introduces typed transaction intents, a deterministic policy verifier, and signed Policy Decision Records (PDRs) that cryptographically bind the approved intent, policy, and simulation report to the exact execution bytes, with replay and expiration protection. A Solidity smart account enforces PDR signatures on-chain with a measured overhead of 29,826-31,822 gas. We evaluate PACE against six baselines on 40 tasks spanning four attack categories plus benign utility (2,800 trials, 10 seeds). In our deterministic sandbox, PACE achieves a 0.00 unsafe execution rate and 0.00 false-positive rate on benign tasks, compared to 0.80 for the unguarded baseline. Ablation studies identify permissive policy settings (+57.5 pp) and the touched-contract allowlist (+12.5 pp) as the dominant safety components. To test whether the same deterministic floor holds for real model outputs, the artifact additionally provides a three-model live-LLM evaluation over the full task suite with repeated runs. A mainnet-fork harness is included for archive-RPC deployments, but fork results are reported only when the corresponding artifacts are generated. These auxiliary studies are separate from, and never substitute for, the deterministic benchmark. We frame our claims as logic-level safety within a reproducible benchmark rather than deployment-ready DeFi security.

</details>


### [242] [COMIC: Reference-Aware Safety Gating for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17234)
*Md Abdullahil Oaphy,Anhao Xiang,Zongxing Xie,Huayue Gu,Chenyu Wang,Honghui Xu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现多模态大模型（MLLMs）存在一种"引用依赖"的安全漏洞：当提示和图像单独看都没有危害，但模型将看似无害的操作（如摘要、翻译）绑定到局部视觉目标时，就会产生不安全行为。针对这一结构性弱点，作者提出COMIC（Context-Operation-Modality-Image-Classifier），一个引用感知的生成前安全门控机制，通过推断操作类型和引用类型、构建候选目标、进行视觉引用解析，并基于显式的"操作-目标"对评估安全性。实验表明COMIC在多个开源MLLM上持续提升鲁棒性，同时保持良性效用和实际效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态安全防御主要对整个提示-图像对进行整体审查，但真正的安全相关单元是模型在引用解析（dereference）过程中产生的"操作-目标"对。这种结构性错配使得现有防御在有害语义被本地化、仅在接地后激活且依赖视觉引用解析时性能显著下降。

Method: COMIC是一个引用感知的生成前安全门控机制，包含以下步骤：1) 推断请求的操作和引用类型；2) 从OCR和开放词汇提议中构建候选目标；3) 对可能指代的对象进行接地（grounding）；4) 在显式的操作-目标对上进行安全评估。为保守处理歧义，COMIC结合最大风险聚合与质量感知路由，最终决定放行或阻止请求。

Result: COMIC在多个开源多模态大模型上、本地化和广泛的多模态越狱基准测试以及良性引用敏感场景中进行了评估。结果表明COMIC持续提升了模型鲁棒性，同时保留了良性效用和实际运行效率。

Conclusion: 多模态安全无法仅通过对提示-图像对进行整体审查来可靠地执行，必须建模请求的操作、该操作所应用的视觉目标以及该接地的置信度，才能有效防御引用依赖型多模态越狱攻击。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interact with screenshots, scanned documents, diagrams, and other visually grounded inputs. This shift introduces a new safety risk: in many multimodal jailbreaks, neither the prompt nor the image is harmful in isolation. Unsafe behavior emerges only when the model binds an apparently benign operation, such as summarizing, translating, or following, to a localized visual target. This reveals a structural weakness in current multimodal defenses, which largely moderate the prompt-image pair as a whole even though the true security-relevant unit is the grounded operation-target pair produced during dereference. In this work, we identify and analyze this reference-dependent failure mode and show that existing defenses degrade when harmful semantics are localized, activated only after grounding, and dependent on visual reference resolution. To address this problem, we propose COMIC (Context-Operation-Modality-Image-Classifier), a reference-aware pre-generation safety gate for MLLMs. COMIC first infers the requested operation and reference type, constructs candidate targets from OCR and open-vocabulary proposals, grounds plausible referents, and evaluates safety over explicit operation-target pairs. To handle ambiguity conservatively, COMIC combines max-risk aggregation with quality-aware routing before deciding whether to forward or block a request. We evaluate COMIC across multiple open-source MLLMs, localized and broader multimodal jailbreak benchmarks, and benign reference-sensitive settings. The results show that COMIC consistently improves robustness while preserving benign utility and practical efficiency. More broadly, our findings suggest that multimodal safety cannot be enforced reliably without modeling the requested operation, the visual target to which it applies, and the confidence of that grounding.

</details>


### [243] [ADAPTD: Adaptive Detection and Proactive Threat Defense for Autonomous APT attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17251)
*Yeongwoo Kim,Quanyan Zhu,György Dán*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ADAPTD，一个通信与计算高效的决策理论框架，用于检测和遏制企业分段网络中的高级持续性威胁（APT）横向传播。该框架结合了紧凑杀伤链、即时阻断机制和预测性驱逐策略，在实验验证中展现了优于扩散HMM的信念更新性能、相比Transformer更少的误驱逐、噪声环境下的自适应遏制能力，并通过消融研究证明了双重防御行动的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APT攻击者越来越多地采用复杂技术在分段企业网络中横向传播。及时检测与防御依赖于跨子网协调，但维持全局态势感知会产生大量通信开销。需要在灵活监控与适应性遏制之间进行权衡管理，以降低防御成本并减少不必要的干扰。

Method: ADAPTD框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 紧凑杀伤链用于识别多样化攻击向量；(2) 即时阻断机制实现及时遏制；(3) 预测性驱逐策略恢复系统安全。框架基于决策理论，采用去中心化信念更新方案，以通信与计算高效的方式实现全局态势感知。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 去中心化信念更新方案优于最先进的扩散HMM；(2) ADAPTD相比基于Transformer的检测大幅减少误驱逐；(3) 在噪声环境下，自适应阻断能有效遏制攻击者同时最小化不必要的中断；(4) 消融研究证实结合两种防御行动能显著降低防御者的总成本。

Conclusion: ADAPTD在通信效率、检测准确性和防御成本控制方面均展现出优势，其结合即时阻断与预测性驱逐的双重防御策略为分段企业网络中的APT横向传播防御提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Advanced persistent threat (APT) actors increasingly employ sophisticated techniques to propagate laterally through segmented enterprise networks. Timely detection and defense depend on cross-subnetwork coordination, yet maintaining global situational awareness generates substantial communication overhead. To manage this tradeoff, flexible monitoring and adaptable containment are imperative. This paper presents ADAPTD, a communication- and computation-efficient, decision-theoretic framework integrating: (i) compact kill chains for identifying diverse attack vectors, (ii) an immediate blocking mechanism for timely containment, and (iii) a predictive eviction strategy to restore system security. Our experiments validate ADAPTD's effectiveness across diverse threat scenarios. First, our decentralized belief update scheme outperforms state-of-the-art diffusion HMM. Second, ADAPTD substantially reduces false evictions compared to transformer-based detection. Third, under noisy environments, adaptive blocking contains attackers while minimizing unnecessary disruption. Lastly, the ablation study confirms that combining two defensive actions significantly reduces the defender's total cost.

</details>


### [244] [When Agents Act on Web3: An Attack-Surface Survey of MCP, Skills, and Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17275)
*Rabimba Karanjai,Yang Lu,Nour Diallo,Wujie Xiong,Lei Xu,Weidong,Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了AI智能体在MCP生态中通过区块链执行层行使工具权限时面临的独特安全威胁，提出四个关键属性（不可逆性、签名授权、持续自主性、序列级组合）从根本上改变了威胁模型。作者构建了攻击面分类法和Web3风险映射矩阵，发现现有防御措施仅能阻止不到30%的攻击，模型级安全拒绝率低于3%，并据此提出研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体从"读取"转向"执行"（MCP生态中修改外部状态的工具使用占比从27%升至65%），智能体在公链上行使权限时，攻击后果由区块链执行层决定而非传统软件假设。通用智能体安全中的可恢复性失败在链上变成不可逆损失，现有安全文献碎片化且缺乏系统性威胁模型分析。

Method: 本文采用综述与分析方法：(1) 主张区块链执行层的四个属性（不可逆性、签名授权、持续自主性、序列级组合）质变威胁模型；(2) 将碎片化的MCP安全文献组织为攻击面分类法；(3) 构建Web3风险映射矩阵，将每类攻击与放大影响、责任放大器、代表缓解措施及残留缺口相关联；(4) 综合防御机制（含新兴区块链机制）并评估其有效性。

Result: 研究发现：区块链执行层属性使可恢复的通用智能体安全失败转变为持续不可逆损失；现有防御措施在改善但远不充足——测量表明防护仅能阻止不到30%的攻击，模型级安全拒绝率低于3%。

Conclusion: 现有安全体系不足以应对区块链上AI智能体的权限滥用风险，需从风险矩阵的空白格中提炼研究议程，推动面向区块链执行层的专门安全机制发展。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act rather than merely read: across the Model Context Protocol (MCP) ecosystem, the share of deployed tools that modify external state has risen from 27% to 65% of tool use. When agents exercise this authority on public blockchains through MCP, skills, and tool calling, the consequences of an attack are governed by the blockchain execution layer rather than by conventional software assumptions. This survey argues that four properties of that layer (irreversibility, signing authority, continuous autonomy, and sequence-level composition) qualitatively change the threat model, turning the recoverable failures of generic agent security into a standing, irreversible loss. We organize the fragmented MCP-security literature into an attack-surface taxonomy, then contribute a Web3 risk-mapping matrix that ties each attack class to its amplified impact, the responsible amplifiers, a representative mitigation, and the residual gap. We synthesize defenses, including emerging blockchain-based mechanisms, and find them improving but insufficient: measured protections stop fewer than 30% of attacks, and model-level safety refuses fewer than 3%. We close by positioning the work against adjacent surveys and deriving a research agenda from the matrix's open cells.

</details>


### [245] [FlowShield: cryptocurrency anti-money laundering with transaction semantics parsing and fund flow tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17355)
*Qishuang Fu,Andreas Deppeler,Joseph K. Liu,Yixin Liu,Shirui Pan,Qin Wang,Weiqing Wang,Tsz Hon Yuen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FlowShield是一个加密货币反洗钱（Crypto AML）框架，通过恢复行为级语义、从三个互补视角重建资金流子图、融合LLM编码的文本语义与GCN编码的图结构信息，实现交易级洗钱检测，并生成面向调查人员的可读可疑活动报告（SARs）。同时构建并开源了首个公开的多链洗钱数据集BybitML，平均F1分数达98.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Crypto AML方法存在三个主要缺陷：过分简化交易语义、依赖拓扑中心信号、仅输出孤立的检测标签，难以应对跨区块链、多语义、快速碎片化资产的复杂洗钱行为。

Method: FlowShield包含三个核心步骤：(1) 从可观测关系中恢复行为级语义，使洗钱意图显式化；(2) 从三个互补视角重建资金流子图以追踪价值来源和再分配；(3) 采用文本-结构融合机制，让LLM编码的语义与GCN编码的结构进行交互，最终生成可读的SARs报告。

Result: 在BybitML和两个公开洗钱数据集上的实验表明，FlowShield取得了最佳整体性能，平均F1分数达到98.0%。行为分析和SAR分析进一步证明其能揭示多样化的洗钱策略并生成可用于调查复杂多跳资金流的可读报告。

Conclusion: FlowShield通过融合语义理解和结构学习，在提升检测精度的同时增强了可解释性，为加密货币反洗钱提供了一种全新的检测与报告生成一体化范式，并为多链洗钱研究提供了首个公开基准数据集。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency anti-money laundering (Crypto AML) is increasingly challenged by sophisticated laundering behaviors that rapidly fragment stolen assets through diverse semantics and across multiple blockchains. Existing Crypto AML methods often simplify transaction semantics, rely on topology-centric signals, or output isolated detection labels. In this paper, we present \textsc{FlowShield}, a Crypto AML framework for transaction-level laundering detection and investigator-facing report generation. \textsc{FlowShield} first recovers behavior-level semantics from observable relations, making laundering intents explicit. To trace value provenance and redistribution, \textsc{FlowShield} reconstructs fund-flow subgraphs from three complementary perspectives. It then employs a text--structure fusion mechanism, enabling the interplay between large language model (LLM)-encoded semantics and flow texts with graph convolutional network (GCN)-encoded structure. Beyond mere detection, \textsc{FlowShield} further generates readable suspicious activity reports (SARs), offering investigators concise summaries and explainable red flags. To address the data scarcity in multi-chain detection, we construct and open-source \textit{BybitML}, the first public multi-chain laundering dataset. We evaluate \textsc{FlowShield} on \textit{BybitML} and two public laundering datasets and experimental results demonstrate that \textsc{FlowShield} achieves the best overall performance, with an average F1 score of 98.0\%. Further behavior and SAR analyses demonstrate that \textsc{FlowShield} can reveal diverse laundering strategies and produce readable reports for investigating complex multi-hop fund flows.

</details>


### [246] [Fair ASR: Re-Evaluating Black-Box Jailbreaks under Shared Target-Call Budgets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17360)
*Zhida He,Xiaoyu Wen,Han Qi,Ziyuan Zhou,Peng Yu,Jiajia Li,Chaochao Lu,Qiaosheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Fair-ASR协议，用目标调用次数作为黑盒越狱攻击的统一预算衡量标准，重新评估11种攻击后发现排名随预算变化，简单方法在高预算下竞争力强；基于此效率差距，进一步提出组合式高效攻击ReCode，在GPT-5上以20次目标调用实现85%的ASR，且平均仅需7.19次攻击者调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱攻击评估仅依赖攻击成功率（ASR），忽略了其对攻击预算的依赖性，导致不同方法间比较不公平；而现有的计算感知评估将异构资源折算为FLOPs，对黑盒模型难以估计，无法反映资源特定约束。

Method: 提出Fair-ASR评估协议，以目标调用次数为统一且可直接观测的比较基准（黑盒场景下方法无关），同时单独追踪攻击者调用次数以分析效率；基于Fair-ASR的评估结果，进一步提出ReCode组合攻击方法，融合脱敏重写与两种低成本原语。

Result: 在Fair-ASR协议下重新评估11种代表性攻击发现：攻击排名随目标调用预算显著变化；简单随机扰动和手工模板在同等目标访问下仍具竞争力；没有LLM驱动的攻击在目标调用和攻击者调用两方面都高效。ReCode在20次目标调用预算下对GPT-5达到85% ASR，平均仅需7.19次攻击者调用。

Conclusion: Fair-ASR提供了更公平的黑盒越狱攻击评估基准，揭示了现有方法的效率差距；ReCode展示了在目标调用和攻击者调用双维度上均衡高效的可能性，为后续越狱攻击与防御研究提供了新的评估范式。

Abstract: Reliable jailbreak evaluation is essential for assessing LLM safety, but most existing studies rely solely on attack success rate (ASR) without accounting for its dependence on attack budgets, resulting in unfair comparisons across methods. Existing compute-aware evaluations reduce heterogeneous resources into FLOPs, which is difficult to estimate for black-box models and fails to capture resource-specific constraints. To provide a comparable evaluation basis, we introduce Fair-ASR, an evaluation protocol for black-box jailbreak attacks under shared target-call budgets B, using target calls as a directly observable and method-agnostic comparison axis while tracking attacker calls separately for efficiency analysis. We re-evaluate 11 representative attacks under the Fair-ASR protocol and find that attack rankings change substantially across target-call budgets, simple stochastic perturbations and hand-crafted templates remain highly competitive under equal target access, and no evaluated LLM-driven method is efficient in both target and attacker calls. Motivated by this efficiency gap, we introduce ReCode, a compositional budget-efficient attack that combines desensitization rewriting with two effective low-cost primitives identified by Fair-ASR. Under a budget of 20 target calls, ReCode achieves 85% ASR on GPT-5 while requiring only 7.19 attacker calls per request on average, showing strong efficiency in both target and attacker calls.

</details>


### [247] [Trusted Workflow Relays:Cross-Tenant Email Abuse and Composable Red Team Initial-Access Primitives in Multi-Tenant Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17361)
*Priyank Nigam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了云应用中"可信工作流中继"（trusted workflow relay）漏洞：认证用户利用提供商的邮件发送服务跨租户发送消息，绕过应用层授权限制，类似于SMTP开放中继但发生在应用层栈之上。作者披露了三个真实漏洞案例，并提出了测试矩阵和缓解控制措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云应用通过提供商运营的邮件身份发送通知，虽然提高了投递率，但将通知参数的提供者与实际发件服务主体分离，导致应用层授权失效，认证攻击者可跨租户控制受信任服务发送的内容，构成新型钓鱼攻击面。

Method: 通过三个负责任披露并修复的跨租户通知工作流漏洞案例进行分析；定义"可信工作流中继"概念；构建通知管道测试矩阵；将原语映射到MITRE ATT&CK无附件钓鱼技术；与RFC 8628设备码钓鱼建立关联。

Result: 发现三类漏洞：（1）后端绕过UI长度限制，HTML/CSS注入，可隐藏服务控制文本；（2）缺少收件人租户验证加上攻击者控制的主题和HTML字段；（3）审批应用中的弱访问控制、顺序对象标识符、缺失操作授权和不完整令牌验证，将通知滥用与授权失败组合。

Conclusion: SPF、DKIM、DMARC等邮件认证机制无法验证应用层发送是否被授权。建议采用租户绑定、类型化模板、对象级授权、令牌受众验证和身份遥测等控制措施来缓解此类攻击。

Abstract: Cloud applications routinely send notifications through provider-operated mail identities, which improves deliverability but separates the actor who supplies notification parameters from the service principal that originates the message. In three responsibly disclosed and remediated cross-tenant notification workflows, an authenticated actor could reach recipients across tenant boundaries and, to varying degrees, control content that a trusted provider service delivered. In the first, backend requests bypassed a UI length limit, raw HTML and CSS survived into the delivered message, attacker links rendered, and CSS could hide service-controlled text; iframes and non-web URI schemes were rejected. The second combined missing recipient-tenant validation with attacker-controlled subject and HTML fields. The third, an approval application, added weak access control, sequential object identifiers, missing action authorization, and incomplete token validation, composing notification abuse with authorization failures.
  The pattern is analogous to a classical unauthenticated SMTP open relay, but the failure has moved up the stack: the actor is authenticated and the provider is the legitimate sender, yet application-layer authorization still fails to constrain who may cause it to send what to whom. We define a trusted workflow relay as a delivered, service-authentic message for which the application-level send-authorization predicate is false. We give a test matrix for notification pipelines, map the primitive to MITRE ATT&CK techniques for attachment-free phishing, and link it to device-code phishing (RFC 8628). SPF, DKIM, and DMARC can authenticate a message yet cannot establish that an application-level send was authorized. We conclude with controls for tenant binding, typed templates, object-level authorization, token audience validation, and identity telemetry.

</details>


### [248] [FESC: Remodeling Long-Context Private Inference with Encrypted State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17442)
*Yufan Zhu,Chao Jin,Khin Mi Mi Aung,Xiaokui Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FESC是一个混合FHE-MPC系统，用于私有长上下文选择性SSM推理，通过因子化扫描-收缩方法避免密集展开，首次在单GPU上实现L≥1,024的原生端到端私有推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理长敏感文档需要高效且隐私保护的长上下文推理。现有私有推理系统优化或分发加密Transformer注意力，但其二次方token-pair计算在序列增长时成为瓶颈。选择性SSM提供线性时间递归，但直接加密实现存在线性乘法深度、序列级状态驻留或密集FHE-MPC转换等问题。

Method: 提出FESC（Factorized Encrypted Scan-Contract）混合FHE-MPC系统：采用因子化扫描-收缩方法在转换边界保持输入相关转换紧凑、避免密集展开、按需流式传输状态块并在转换前收缩输出；针对Mamba-2实例设计了GPU优化的CKKS内核用于线性计算，以及用于SiLU、softplus、指数和RMSNorm的MPC协议，并采用近似感知微调。

Result: FESC是首个在单GPU上以L≥1,024完成原生端到端私有长文档推理的系统。在L=2,048时，12层Mamba-base模型在单张A100 GPU上77.3分钟内完成推理，峰值内存32.7GB，同时在长文档任务上保持接近明文精度。

Conclusion: FESC通过因子化扫描-收缩策略解决了选择性SSM私有推理中的效率难题，实现了长文档私有推理的可行性和可扩展性，为私有长上下文机器学习推理提供了新路径。

Abstract: Processing long, sensitive documents with machine-learning models requires efficient, privacy-preserving long-context inference. Prior private inference systems optimize or distribute encrypted Transformer attention, but its quadratic token-pair work remains the bottleneck as sequence length grows. Selective state-space models (SSMs) offer linear-time recurrence, yet direct encrypted implementation incurs linear multiplicative depth, sequence-wide state residency, or dense FHE-MPC conversion. We present Factorized Encrypted Scan-Contract (FESC), a hybrid FHE-MPC system for private long-context selective SSM inference. Its factorized scan-contract keeps input-dependent transitions compact across conversion boundaries, composes them without dense expansion, streams state chunks on demand, and contracts outputs before conversion. We demonstrate interface compatibility of the scan-contract implementation across invariant and selective SSM architectures. For our Mamba-2 instantiation, we design GPU-optimized CKKS kernels for linear computations, MPC protocols for SiLU, softplus, exponential, and RMSNorm, with approximation-aware fine-tuning. To our knowledge, FESC is the first private long-document inference system to complete native end-to-end execution at $L \geq 1{,}024$ on a single GPU. At $L = 2{,}048$, a 12-layer Mamba-base model completes inference in 77.3 minutes on one A100 GPU with a peak memory footprint of 32.7 GB, while maintaining near-plaintext accuracy on the evaluated long-document tasks.

</details>


### [249] [Decomposition Attacks Across Unlinkable Identities: Limits of Stateful Defenses for LLM Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17445)
*Bowen Sun,Zhengyue Zhao,Xiaogeng Liu,Yinzhi Cao,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型服务如何防御分解攻击。研究表明，在攻击者使用不可关联身份且可重试的条件下，仅依赖状态监控无法有效防御分解攻击。实验证明所有十种防御策略（包括具备精确映射的特权策略）要么无法阻止攻击，要么超出误拒预算。有效防御需要额外的身份绑定、成本控制或答案使用管控等机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型服务大多采用无状态防御，仅对单个请求进行判断，容易被分解攻击绕过——攻击者将有害任务拆分为单独的合规请求，再合并答案实现攻击。防御分解攻击需要状态化监控，但如果攻击者使用不可关联的身份并在他处合并答案，则缺乏可靠的请求分组信号。本文旨在探索在这种困难设定下，分解攻击是否仍可被阻止。

Method: 本文采用理论分析与实证验证相结合的方法。理论上，针对无重试的固定攻击策略，证明安全性与效用性的权衡完全取决于良性请求如何被分组——持久且可识别的分组允许有效防御，而全新且不可区分的分组则无法防御。实证上，在91个可执行任务和11,393个能力匹配的良性请求上测试了十种防御策略，包括一种具备精确请求-操作映射的特权策略，在1%良性请求拒绝上限和0.5%背景流量拒绝上限下评估其防御效果。

Result: 实验结果表明，在给定的预算限制下，所有十种测试策略要么无法阻止分解攻击，要么超出误拒预算。对于防御未见过的任务家族，攻击成功率在尝试一次后至少为99%，尝试两次后达到100%。这证实了在有重试和反馈学习条件下，有用的安全操作点会消失。

Conclusion: 仅依赖请求分组的状态化监控不足以防御分解攻击，尤其是在攻击者可以重试并从放行/阻止决策中学习的情况下。有效的防御必须依赖于与分组相关的额外证据或机制，例如可靠的身份关联、新身份的成本代价，或对答案使用的控制权。

Abstract: Most large language model services use stateless defenses, which judge only the current request, to refuse harmful tasks. Decomposition attacks exploit this limitation by splitting a harmful task into individually permissible requests and combining their answers. Defending against them therefore requires a stateful monitor that considers requests together. If it can group all requests for one attacker task, it can stop the attack. However, attackers can use unlinkable identities and combine answers elsewhere, leaving no reliable grouping signal. We ask whether decomposition attacks can still be stopped under this setting. For a fixed attack strategy without retries, we prove that the achievable security and utility tradeoff depends entirely on how benign requests for the same capabilities are grouped. Persistent, recognizable groups permit a useful defense; fresh, indistinguishable groups do not. When attackers can retry and learn from Allow/Block decisions, this useful operating point disappears: the feedback reveals what passes but not whether a block was correct. Experiments on 91 executable tasks and 11,393 capability-matched benign requests support these results. Under a 1% denial cap for these requests and a 0.5% cap for unrelated background traffic, all ten tested policies, including one privileged policy with an exact request-to-operation map, either fail to stop attacks or exceed the budget. On defense-unseen task families, attack success is at least 99% after one attempt and 100% after two. Effective defenses therefore require additional evidence or mechanisms tied to grouping, such as reliable identity linkage, costs for fresh identities, or control over answer use.

</details>


### [250] [KeyPooling: Measuring Where LLM API Relay Paths Collapse Prompt Cache Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17485)
*Bowen Sun,Yixi Cai,Xiaogeng Liu,Zhengyue Zhao,Yinzhi Cao,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出KeyPooling测量方法，揭示了LLM API中继网关在共享上游凭据下存在跨客户缓存读取的安全漏洞。研究表明，在5个开源网关连接OpenAI和Anthropic时，默认情况下均未将客户绑定到上游凭据，导致跨客户缓存泄露。研究者还提出了防御契约：每个客户必须进入提供商强制的域，或确保从认证身份派生的命名空间在所有最终缓存查找和写入中存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型API中继网关分别认证客户，但通常通过共享的提供商凭据转发请求。提供商将提示缓存作用域绑定到上游主体和命名空间，因此映射到同一缓存身份的中继客户可以观察彼此的缓存状态。先前研究仅显示选定端点的缓存共享，但未识别哪个凭据、池、适配器或嵌套跳控制最终身份，需要系统性的测量方法。

Method: 作者提出KeyPooling测量方法，通过缓存查找和写入追踪客户身份，验证运行时转换，并一次测试一个预测的身份组件。该方法连接OpenAI和Anthropic，在5个开源网关上进行测试，并通过OpenRouter框架在一周内覆盖80.5%的符合条件的token量。

Result: 5个网关在共享凭据下均暴露跨客户缓存读取（对两个提供商都是如此）。OpenRouter测试发现28个标签中12个存在跨账户读取，承载33.7%的流量。在一条生产路由上，受控程序恢复了8个连续目标位置而无需目标访问。布防防御方案（将拆分放在可复用公共前缀之后）在1.7-2.5%的成本增加下保留了大部分建模复用。

Conclusion: 作者得出防御契约：每个客户必须进入提供商强制的域，或从认证身份派生的命名空间必须在所有最终缓存查找和写入中存活。论文还指出缓存粒度、路由、速率限制、归因和预算应被视为token逐字恢复的条件而非安全控制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) API relays authenticate customers separately but often forward requests through shared provider credentials. Providers scope prompt caches to upstream principals and namespaces, so relay customers mapped to one cache identity can observe each other's cache state. Prior work showed cache sharing at selected endpoints but did not identify which credential, pool, adapter, or nested hop controls the finalidentity. We present KeyPooling, a measurement method that traces customer identity through cache lookup and write, verifies runtime transformations, and tests one predicted identity component at a time. Across five open-source gateways connected to OpenAI and Anthropic, none bound customers to upstream credentials by default; under a shared credential, all five exposed cross-customer cache reads for both providers. Principal and namespace splits, pool associations, and adapter and nested-relay contrasts localized the controlling transformations. In an outcome-independent weekly OpenRouter frame, tests covered 80.5% of eligible token volume and found cross-account reads for 12 of 28 labels carrying 33.7% of volume. On one production route, a controlled procedure recovered eight consecutive target positions without target access. Broader tests identify cache granularity, routing, rate limits, attribution, and budget as conditions for token-by-token recovery, not security controls. We derive a defense contract: every customer must enter a provider-enforced domain, or a namespace derived from authenticated identity must survive every final cache lookup and write. Placing this split after reusable public prefixes preserved most modeled reuse at a 1.7-2.5% cost increase.

</details>


### [251] [Cross-Domain Joint DDoS Detection in Multi-Controller SDN via Confidence-Based Entropy Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17507)
*Zhaoyang Zhang,Shen Wang,Xiaofeng Tao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 这篇论文发现多控制器SDN中基于熵的DDoS检测存在"聚合偏差"问题——攻击后期聚合控制器持续产生过多误报，而边缘控制器已恢复正常。作者提出跨域置信度融合框架，利用轻量级边缘消息校准聚合控制器决策，将误报率从8.87%降至1.96%，F1分数从89.04%提升至96.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多控制器SDN中DDoS攻击呈现"分散源、集中目标"的跨域模式，熵检测器从单控制器直接迁移到多控制器场景时会出现被忽视的聚合偏差问题，导致系统检测精度显著下降。

Method: 提出跨域置信度融合框架，利用边缘控制器的轻量级消息（不共享原始流量数据）来校准聚合控制器的检测决策；该框架具有非侵入性、通信高效和可增量部署的特点。通过系统实验识别出聚合偏差源于OpenFlow统计延迟与无约束动态阈值漂移的耦合效应。

Result: 在三控制器线性Mininet测试平台（24台主机、10次运行）上验证，该方法在保持边缘控制器性能的同时，将聚合控制器误报率从8.87%降低到1.96%，F1分数从89.04%提升到96.89%。

Conclusion: 提出的跨域置信度融合框架能有效解决多控制器SDN中熵检测器的聚合偏差问题，是一种实用且可扩展的解决方案，且无需共享原始数据即可实现跨域协作检测。

Abstract: In multi-controller Software-Defined Networking (SDN), Distributed Denial-of-Service (DDoS) attacks exhibit a "dispersed source, concentrated target" pattern across domains, i.e., attack traffic originates from multiple edge-controller domains but converges on a victim in a single aggregation controller domain. While entropy-based DDoS detectors are effective in single-controller settings, their direct application in multi-controller SDN reveals a previously overlooked anomaly. Through systematic experiments, we identify an aggregation bias: during the post-attack transition phase, the aggregation controller continues to generate excessive false positives, while edge controllers have already returned to normal. We attribute this phenomenon to the coupled effects of OpenFlow statistics lag and unconstrained dynamic-threshold drift. To address this issue, we propose a cross-domain confidence-fusion framework that leverages lightweight edge-side messages to calibrate aggregation-controller decisions without sharing raw traffic data. The framework is non-intrusive, communication-efficient, and incrementally deployable. Experiments on a three-controller linear Mininet testbed with 24 hosts over 10 runs show that the method preserves edge-controller performance while reducing the aggregation false positive rate from 8.87% to 1.96% and increasing the F1 score from 89.04% to 96.89%.

</details>


### [252] [CryptDough: A Unified Analytics Engine for Secure Multiparty Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17529)
*Muhammad Faisal,Alessandra Lanz,Sam Buxbaum,Adam Godel,Vasiliki Kalavri,Mayank Varia,John Liagouris*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CryptDough是一个统一的MPC安全多方计算分析引擎，支持多种威胁模型和跨领域分析工作负载（关系型、时序、机器学习推理），通过分层系统设计和虚拟向量抽象，使用户能够编写单线程代码，同时将通信、并行化和内存管理复杂性下推到执行引擎，性能上通常比现有系统快2倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MPC解决方案通常只支持单一的威胁模型或工作负载类型，缺乏一个能够在同一系统运行时内支持跨领域分析（关系型、时序、ML推理）和多种威胁模型的统一引擎。

Method: CryptDough采用（i）分层系统设计，通过渐进式抽象降低实现模块化和可扩展性；（ii）虚拟向量概念，允许用户在所有软件栈层编写单线程代码，将通信、并行化和内存管理的复杂度推送到执行引擎处理。

Result: CryptDough在功能上泛化了最先进的MPC系统，并在其支持的各类分析任务上保持竞争力，性能通常超过现有系统2倍以上。

Conclusion: CryptDough通过统一的设计框架有效解决了MPC系统在威胁模型和工作负载类型上的碎片化问题，在保持功能通用性的同时实现了显著的性能优势。

Abstract: We present CryptDough, a unified analytics engine for secure multiparty computation (MPC). CryptDough enables multiple distrusting parties to jointly execute a data analysis pipeline on their private inputs and learn nothing beyond the result (e.g., aggregate statistics). Unlike existing MPC solutions that support a single threat model or workload type, CryptDough provides built-in support for cross-domain analytics (relational, time series, ML inference) under various threat models, all within the same system runtime.
  CryptDough contributes (i) a hierarchical system design that facilitates modularity and extensibility through progressive lowering of abstractions, and (ii) the concept of virtual vectors that enable users to write single-threaded code across all layers of the software stack, while pushing the complexity of communication, parallelization, and memory management down to the execution engine. We show that CryptDough generalizes the functionality of state-of-the-art MPC systems and remains competitive on the analytics they support, often outperforming them by more than $2\times$.

</details>


### [253] [SoK: Cross-Chain Transaction Identification and Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17532)
*Hang Zheng,Qishuang Fu,Joseph Liu,Qin Wang,Weiqing Wang,Tsz Hon Yuen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对跨链交易识别与匹配进行了系统化知识整理（SoK），将存款/取款识别方法分为四类，将交易匹配方法归纳为三种机制，评估了现有数据集与工件的可用性（不足半数可获取），并提炼出四个开放挑战以推动可审计、可复现、可操作的跨链分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨链桥、即时加密货币交易所等跨链平台频繁遭受高价值攻击并成为跨链洗钱渠道，而跨链交易比单链交易更难分析：没有任何单一账本记录完整的跨链转账，其证据分散在源链、目标链及链下系统，且不同系统中证据的可用性和可靠性差异很大。

Method: 采用系统化知识整理（SoK）方法：首先将存款与取款识别方法分类为四种方法，将交易匹配方法分类为三种机制（确定性标识符匹配、字段约束启发式、模型辅助匹配）；其次评估现有数据集和工件的可获取性；最后梳理下游攻击检测与资金追踪的支撑情况。

Result: 发现识别与匹配方法的适用性和报告性能主要取决于底层系统所暴露的证据；现有数据集和工件中不足半数仍可获取，并提炼出三种工件失效模式。

Conclusion: 提出了四个开放挑战，旨在实现可审计、可复现且可操作的跨链分析。

Abstract: Cross-chain bridges, instant cryptocurrency exchanges, and centralized cross-ledger platforms move assets across an increasingly multi-chain ecosystem. However, these systems have repeatedly become targets of high-value attacks and channels for cross-chain money laundering. Cross-chain transactions are substantially harder to analyze than single-chain transactions: no single ledger records an entire cross-chain transfer, its evidence is scattered across the source chain, the destination chain, and off-chain systems, and the availability and reliability of that evidence vary widely across systems. In this paper, we present a systematization of knowledge (SoK) on cross-chain transaction identification and matching. First, we classify deposit and withdrawal identification methods into four approaches and transaction matching methods into three mechanisms: deterministic identifier matching, field-constraint heuristics, and model-assisted matching. We find that their applicability and reported performance are shaped mainly by the evidence the underlying system exposes, and we further examine how matched pairs support downstream attack detection and fund tracing. Second, we assess the availability of existing datasets and artifacts, finding that fewer than half remain obtainable, and distill three artifact failure modes. Finally, we outline four open challenges toward auditable, reproducible, and actionable cross-chain analysis.

</details>


### [254] [TENET: Telegram Mini App (in)security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17538)
*Andrea Ciccotelli,Federico Zappone,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了Telegram Mini Apps生态系统中客户端明文存储认证材料（如会话令牌和钱包助记词）的安全漏洞，提出了专用审计工具TENET，对61个Mini Apps进行筛查后发现30个存在安全缺陷，并推动Telegram官方实施了新的安全存储API。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Telegram Mini Apps拥有超过4.5亿日活用户，但其生态存在独特的危险组合：无平台级安全审查、无存储访问限制、用户涉及加密货币资产交易、WebView环境安全保护弱于独立浏览器，导致用户的认证材料和钱包助记词面临严重泄露风险。

Method: 作者开发了TENET审计工具，其设计基于目标秘密的结构属性（模式选择、熵阈值、字符集验证），并基于真实数据集进行实证验证。采用基于流行度的分层、加权抽样策略筛选了61个Mini Apps，其中37个满足处理标准并进行了分析。

Result: 在分析的37个应用中，30个存在安全漏洞，分为三个严重级别：明文存储、可恢复加密和可重放令牌。即使是Telegram官方Wallet也存在严重漏洞可能导致账户完全被攻陷。负责任披露后，Telegram实施了两种新的安全存储API，事后验证确认官方Wallet不再明文暴露恢复助记词。

Conclusion: Telegram Mini Apps生态存在普遍且严重的安全风险，需要平台开发者和第三方Mini App创建者共同采取缓解措施和最佳实践来提升安全性。

Abstract: Telegram, with over 450 million daily active users, has introduced Mini Apps---web-based applications running directly within its client. However, this integration introduces notable security risks. As we demonstrate, many Mini Apps store authentication materials---such as session tokens and wallet mnemonic phrases---in plaintext on client devices, exposing users to unauthorized access, impersonation, and financial exploitation. While insecure client-side storage is a known risk in web applications, the Telegram Mini App ecosystem presents a uniquely dangerous combination of factors absent from prior work: no platform-level security review, no storage access restrictions, a financially motivated user base handling live cryptocurrency assets, and a WebView environment that offers weaker protections than standalone browsers. To investigate this threat, we present TENET, a purpose-built auditing tool whose design decisions---pattern selection, entropy thresholds, and charset validation---are grounded in the structural properties of the secrets targeted and empirically validated against a ground-truth dataset. We screened 61 Mini Apps using a stratified, popularity-weighted sampling strategy based on popularity. Of the 37 applications that met our processing criteria and were analyzed, 30 exhibited security flaws, which we classify into three severity tiers: plaintext storage, recoverable encryption, and replayable tokens. Notably, even Telegram's official Wallet exhibits a severe vulnerability that may lead to full account compromise. Following our responsible disclosure, Telegram implemented two new secure-storage APIs, and our post-remediation verification confirmed that its official Wallet no longer exposes the recovery mnemonic in plaintext. Finally, we propose mitigation measures and best practices for both Telegram platform developers and third-party Mini App creators.

</details>


### [255] [Software Defined Networks Key Relay for Large-Scale Quantum Key Distribution Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17539)
*Stephan Laschet,Gergely Lendvay,Thomas Lorünser,Paul James,Luca Torresetti,Alessandro Colombo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用软件定义网络（SDN）技术编排大规模量子密钥分发网络（QKDN），提出了基于Dijkstra和最大最小容量算法的多路径选择方案，并引入了 oblivious 多方协议用于跨域场景下的中继路径选择，以保护各提供商QKDN的敏感信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模量子密钥分发网络面临编排和路径选择的挑战，需要一种可扩展、有韧性且能互操作的网络管理方案，同时保护跨域场景中各提供商的敏感信息。

Method: 基于ETSI和ITU规范构建QKDN编排架构，由SDN控制器聚合网络KPI并选择最优路径；提出基于Dijkstra和最大最小容量算法的多路径选择算法并内置负载均衡；引入oblivious多方协议用于跨域中继路径选择。

Result: 通过仿真测试了多种路径选择算法的性能并讨论了各自的权衡；探讨了查询批处理、多路径选择和组密钥能力等关键问题，旨在增强网络的可扩展性、韧性和互操作性。

Conclusion: 这些贡献为量子安全网络基础设施提供了增强可扩展性、韧性和互操作性的编排方案，尤其通过隐私保护的跨域路径选择机制解决了多提供商场景下的信任问题。

Abstract: This work addresses the orchestration of large-scale Quantum Key Distribution Networks (QKDNs) using Software Defined Networking (SDN). Building on ETSI and ITU specifications, common best practices and architectures are outlined. The main task of the SDN Controller is to aggregate technical key performance indicators (KPI) from the network and, based on these, select the optimal path. Multiple path selection algorithms, based on Dijkstra or a maximum-minimum capacity algorithm, with built-in load balancing are presented. The algorithms were tested in simulations and their performances, and tradeoffs, are discussed. Additional critical aspects related to SDN controlled QKDNs are discussed, such as query batching, multi-path selection and group key capabilities. An oblivious multi-party protocol is proposed for relay path selection in a multi-domain scenario, so providers don't have to disclose sensitive information about their QKDN. These contributions aim to enhance scalability, resilience and interoperability in quantum-secure network infrastructures.

</details>


### [256] [Reflex-Guard: A Low-Latency Guardrail for LLM Prompt Safety Using Dense Semantic Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17556)
*Istiaque Ahmed,Afia Anjum Borsha,Ranat Das Prangon,Abu-fuad Ahmad,Thi Hong Tran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Reflex-Guard，一个轻量级本地运行的提示词安全过滤护栏，通过越狱感知预处理、紧凑句子变换器嵌入和7个快速二分类器，在37.6ms端到端延迟下实现95.9%的有害提示召回率，远优于Llama Guard 2（255ms）和SafeDecoding（723ms）等现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM护栏方案（如LLM-as-a-judge和云端安全API）虽能检测不安全内容，但每个请求增加250-900ms延迟，无法满足实时应用<100ms的响应需求；同时，将用户提示路由到外部审核端点存在严重的数据隐私问题。

Method: Reflex-Guard包含三个核心组件：越狱感知预处理、紧凑句子变换器嵌入、以及七个快速二分类器。系统在本地运行，使用30,568个来自五个互补来源的策略性平衡数据集进行系统评估。

Result: Reflex-Guard在37.6ms端到端延迟下达到95.9%有害提示召回率；默认阈值下可100%检测GCG后缀攻击和Base64编码提示；DrAttack结构化提示需将阈值降至0.03以获得最优检测；Reflex效率分数（RES）达16.79，优于Llama Guard 2（11.90）和SafeDecoding（9.80）。

Conclusion: 不同攻击类型在嵌入概率空间中占据不同的区域，这为实际部署提供了指导。Reflex-Guard在保持高精度的同时显著降低了延迟，并解决了数据隐私问题，适合实时应用场景。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) in real-world applications often face the risks of specially crafted prompts designed to bypass the safety controls. Existing guardrail methods, such as LLM-as-a-judge and cloud-based safety APIs are able to detect unsafe content. However, they often add a delay of about 250-900 ms to each request. This delay is too high for real-time applications, when the system usually needs to respond in less than 100 ms. Furthermore, routing user prompts through external moderation endpoints raises significant data privacy concerns. This paper introduces Reflex-Guard, a lightweight guardrail that runs locally. It uses jailbreak-aware preprocessing, compact sentence-transformer embeddings, and seven fast binary classifiers. Together, these components enable high-accuracy prompt safety filtering with much lower latency than existing solutions. Through systematic evaluation on a strategically balanced dataset of 30,568 samples drawn from five complementary sources, we demonstrate that Reflex-Guard achieves 95.9% recall on harmful prompts at 37.6 ms end-to-end latency. It is faster than existing baselines, including Llama Guard 2 at 255 ms and SafeDecoding at 723 ms. It can detect 100% of GCG suffix attacks and Base64-encoded prompts using the default threshold. However, DrAttack structured prompts required lowering the threshold to 0.03 for optimal detection, as they produced a distinct probability distribution. Reflex-Guard achieves Reflex Efficiency Score (RES) scores up to 16.79, significantly outperforming Llama Guard 2 (11.90) and SafeDecoding (9.80). This analysis offers practical deployment advice and shows that different attack types occupy distinct regions in the embedding probability space.

</details>


### [257] [HarnessRisk: A Lifecycle-Oriented Benchmark for Agent Harness Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17597)
*Yajing Bai,Jinhao Duan,Jie Peng,Xianfeng Wu,Sijia Liu,Song Wang,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了HarnessRisk，一个面向Agent框架（harness）安全性的生命周期基准测试，涵盖六个运营阶段（框架配置、能力扩展、运行时操作、状态持久化、动作控制、事故恢复），包含128个沙箱化测试用例。实验结果显示攻击成功率在12.6%到80.9%之间，框架配置是最脆弱的阶段，且显式的风险识别并不总能导致安全行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全基准主要针对单一攻击机制或有限的运营场景，难以比较不同框架职责下的安全失败模式，缺乏一个全面的、面向Agent框架多阶段运营的安全性评估基准。

Method: 提出HarnessRisk基准，将Agent框架安全划分为六个运营阶段，构建128个沙箱测试用例（每个用例包含良性用户目标与嵌入在不可信工作流工件中的对抗指令），使用Utility、攻击成功率、持久性和检测性四个指标，在3个框架、6个语言模型、14种模型与框架配置下进行评估。

Result: 攻击成功率在12.6%至80.9%之间，Utility保持在75.0%至97.6%之间；框架配置阶段在所有三个框架中都是最脆弱的；部分配置在超过90%的运行中检测到风险但仍保持了相当大的攻击成功率。

Conclusion: Agent安全性评估需要在多个框架职责层面以及部署的模型-框架配置级别进行，仅依靠风险识别能力不足以保证安全行动，必须同时评估检测与实际行动之间的差距。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed through agent harnesses that manage tools, extensions, persistent state, permissions, and external actions. Existing safety benchmarks mainly target individual attack mechanisms or a limited subset of operational settings, making it difficult to compare how safety failures emerge across different harness responsibilities. We present HarnessRisk, a lifecycle oriented benchmark that organizes agent harness safety into six operational phases including Harness Configuration, Capability Extension, Runtime Operation, State Persistence, Action Control, and Incident Recovery. HarnessRisk contains 128 sandboxed cases, each pairing a benign user objective with an adversarial instruction embedded in an untrusted workflow artifact. We evaluate each trajectory using Utility, Attack Success Rate, Persistence, and Detection. Across three harnesses, six language models, and 14 model and harness configurations, attack success ranges from 12.6% to 80.9%, while Utility remains between 75.0% and 97.6%. Harness Configuration is the most vulnerable phase across all three harnesses, showing that attacks can succeed by altering security sensitive parameters within otherwise authorized workflows. We also find that explicit risk recognition does not reliably lead to safe action, as some configurations detect risks in more than 90% of runs while retaining substantial attack success. These results highlight the need to evaluate agent safety across multiple harness responsibilities and at the level of the deployed model and harness configuration.

</details>


### [258] [An Emulation Anchored Digital Twin Testbed for Cyberattack and Defense Analysis in Hospital IT OT Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17650)
*Prashant Rawat,Ravi Kumar Bairagi,Arunima,Geeta Yadav*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合数字孪生的医院IT/OT网络安全测试平台，用于监控、实验和验证防御机制，实验表明其计算开销极低，并能有效模拟跨域攻击场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代医院依赖IT与OT融合基础设施，但网络安全攻击面扩大，现有测试平台多聚焦于孤立的IT或OT环境，无法真实捕捉跨域医疗交互场景，且难以在真实系统上进行安全验证。

Method: 构建了一个医院IT和OT网络安全测试平台，包括中央服务器、EHR系统、基于SCADA的基础设施以及分割的IT/OT/DMZ网络，支持受控网络攻击执行、软件补丁评估和基于强化学习的防御智能体训练；并扩展为数字孪生，利用日志和网络统计模拟环境实时状态，通过命令执行和容器生命周期编排实现双向交互，支持Modbus/TCP和FHIR/HL7通信协议。

Result: 实验显示计算开销低，平均归一化CPU利用率每容器低于0.4%，多数轻量级服务低于0.01%；OpenPLC Modbus TCP操作中位往返延迟为0.901ms；测试平台成功捕获了从DMZ传播到IT和PLC网络的多阶段SSH攻击。

Conclusion: 该框架为将仿真环境扩展为硬件使能的医院数字孪生奠定了基础，为医院网络安全防御机制的安全验证提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Modern hospitals increasingly rely on integrated Information Technology (IT) and Operational Technology (OT) infrastructures to support critical healthcare services. However, this convergence expands the cybersecurity attack surface and makes safe validation of defensive mechanisms difficult on live systems. Existing testbeds often focus on isolated IT or OT environments and do not capture realistic cross-domain healthcare interactions. This work presents a hospital IT and OT cybersecurity testbed coupled with a digital twin for monitoring, experimentation, and validation of countermeasures. The testbed emulates a central server, Electronic Health Record (EHR) systems, SCADA-based infrastructure, and segmented IT, OT, and DMZ networks. It supports controlled cyberattack execution, software-patch evaluation, and training of RL-based defense agents. The testbed is further extended to a digital twin that models the real-time state of the environment using log and network statistics and enables bidirectional interaction through command execution and container lifecycle orchestration. Modbus/TCP and FHIR/HL7 support realistic communication across healthcare and industrial components. Experimental evaluation shows low computation overhead, with average normalized CPU utilization below 0.4 % per container and most lightweight services operating below 0.01%. OpenPLC Modbus TCP operations achieve a median round-trip latency of 0.901 ms. The testbed also captures a multi-stage SSH-based attack propagating from the DMZ to the IT and PLC networks. The framework provides a foundation for extending the emulated environment toward a hardware-enabled hospital digital twin.

</details>


### [259] [MobileWorldSafety: Benchmarking GUI Agent Safety Against Environmental Injection Attacks in Android Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17659)
*Sujin Chen,Lijun Li,Tianyi Du,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了MobileWorldSafety基准，包含142个基于真实Android应用的风险任务，用于系统评估GUI智能体在环境注入攻击下的安全性。评估了六个智能体，发现攻击成功率高达40.4%~66.9%，表明现有智能体在面对恶意内容时普遍存在安全对齐不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的GUI智能体正从研究原型走向实际部署，但其处理不可信环境内容时极易受到环境注入攻击（包括间接提示注入和对抗性指令）的影响。现有基准缺乏对移动设备上GUI智能体在环境注入攻击下的系统评估，无法覆盖日常用户场景，因此需要建立一个能客观衡量这类安全漏洞的评测基准。

Method: 构建了MobileWorldSafety基准，包含142个基于真实Android应用的风险任务。每个任务定义了一个可编程验证的风险指标，并通过两阶段评估流程：基于规则验证处理明确情况，LLM裁判裁决模糊情况。该方法区分安全失败与能力失败，实现客观可复现的评估。

Result: 对六个智能体（包括通用智能体和专用GUI智能体）的评估表明，所有智能体都高度脆弱，攻击成功率范围为40.4%至66.9%。这表明当前智能体在面对以普通移动环境内容形式呈现的对抗性内容时，往往无法维持安全对齐。

Conclusion: MobileWorldSafety为量化移动GUI智能体的安全漏洞提供了基础，并推动了鲁棒移动GUI智能体的研究发展。当前智能体在环境注入攻击下的表现说明安全对齐仍有显著提升空间。

Abstract: LLM-powered GUI agents that autonomously operate smartphones are rapidly transitioning from research prototypes to early real-world deployment. However, because these agents routinely process untrusted environmental content, they are highly vulnerable to environmental injection attacks, which include indirect prompt injections and adversarial instructions. Such attacks can manipulate the behavior of agents without user awareness through diverse channels encountered in everyday mobile use. Despite these risks, existing benchmarks often fail to capture everyday user scenarios, lacking a systematic evaluation of GUI agents under environmental injection attacks on mobile devices. To address this gap, we introduce MobileWorldSafety, a benchmark of 142 risk tasks built on real Android applications. For each task, we define a programmatically verifiable risk indicator over the final system state and evaluate outcomes with a two-stage pipeline: rule-based verification handles unambiguous cases, while an LLM judge adjudicates ambiguous ones. This distinguishes safety failures from capability failures and enables objective and reproducible assessment. Evaluations on six agents, including both general agents and specialized GUI agents, demonstrate that all agents remain highly vulnerable, with attack success rates ranging from 40.4% to 66.9%. These findings indicate that current agents often fail to maintain safety alignment when adversarial content is presented as ordinary mobile context. MobileWorldSafety provides a foundation for quantifying these vulnerabilities and advancing research on robust mobile GUI agents.

</details>


### [260] [MemCatalyst: Amplifying Data Auditing on Vision-Language Models via Data Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17722)
*Xukun Luan,Jinyan Liu,Yuhui Gong,Yuanguo Bi,Bing Hu,Xuesong Li,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MemCatalyst是一种数据投毒工具集，通过文本投毒（PT）和图像投毒（PI）两种策略，迫使视觉语言模型（VLM）在训练时过度学习图像特征与文本语义之间的不一致性，从而增强成员推理（MI）审计的性能。该方法在黑盒设置下具有跨模型架构的可迁移性，在仅使用少量投毒样本的情况下即可显著提升MI AUC分数，且对模型性能影响极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据持有者（如艺术家）需要判断其数据是否被未经授权用于模型训练，这涉及知识产权和个人隐私问题。虽然成员推理（MI）是直接的数据审计工具，但在视觉语言模型上的审计性能仍有不足，需要增强。

Method: 提出MemCatalyst框架，包含两种投毒策略：文本投毒（PT）和图像投毒（PI）。通过迫使VLM在训练中过度学习图像特征与文本语义之间的特定不一致性，增强模型对成员信息审计的敏感性。同时验证了投毒样本在不同VLM架构间的可迁移性，适用于黑盒场景。

Result: 在两种主流VLM上使用五种最先进的数据审计方法进行了广泛评估。结果表明，MemCatalyst在极少的投毒样本预算下显著提升了MI的AUC分数，同时对模型性能的影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: MemCatalyst有效增强了视觉语言模型上的数据审计能力，为数据持有人提供了一种低成本、高效的工具来验证其数据是否被未经授权使用，且不影响模型正常功能。

Abstract: Vision-Language models (VLMs) achieve outstanding performance largely due to the amount of training data available on the internet. At the same time, data holders (e.g., artists) urgently need to determine whether their data has been used for model training without authorization, which concerns both intellectual property rights and personal privacy. Data auditing, particularly through membership inference (MI), has attracted attention as a direct tool. This work proposes MemCatalyst, a set of data poisoning tools, aiming to amplify the data auditing performance on VLMs. MemCatalyst employs two strategies: Poisoning Text (PT) and Poisoning Image (PI). MemCatalyst forces VLMs to over-learn specific inconsistencies between image features and textual semantics during training, thereby increasing their susceptibility to membership information auditing. Crucially, the transferability of poisoned samples across different VLM architectures is demonstrated to be effective in the black-box setting. Extensive evaluations using five state-of-the-art data audits on two prominent VLMs demonstrate that MemCatalyst markedly enhances MI AUC scores with a minimal budget of poisoned samples, while maintaining a negligible impact on model performance.

</details>


### [261] [BullsEye: Directed Firmware Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17729)
